## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- odh-model-controller (also known as\: ODH Model Controller, omc) is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 1 CRD(s)\: Account.
- deploying 2 workload(s) (odh-model-controller, model-serving-api) with 2 container(s).
- watching 27 resource type(s).
- exposing 3 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8080, 8443.
- with 10 webhook(s) (5 validating, 5 mutating).
- protected by 1 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: s3.
- runtime dependencies\: OpenTelemetry Collector, knative-local-gateway, kubernetes.
- defining 1 API spec type(s) across 4 struct(s) with 18 total fields.
- with 215 operator config constants (1 env vars).
- following 8 reconciliation steps across 8 controller(s).
- with 3 ingress route(s).
- integrates with KServe, Kueue, Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- kustomize overlays\: config/base, config/certmanager, config/crd/external, config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/odh/rbac/openshift, config/overlays/xks/cert-manager, config/overlays/xks, config/prometheus, config/rbac, config/runtimes, config/runtimes/vllm, config/runtimes/vllm-omni, config/runtimes-fast-1, config/runtimes-fast-2, config/server, config/webhook.
- references components\: kserve (import), kubeflow (import).
